The no-collapse statement no longer rests on "unbranched" at all; it rests on "branched but indistinguishable to order δ²," a quantitative claim conditional on a reading. "Never resolved" reads "not resolved at any observable rate," on the continuous reading.

**Standing.** Candidate, corrected. One cost closed (branched h, branched sink). One cost replaced: whether the free stock is continuous or granular in ħ, which decides Γ = δ²ω or δω; entered as an open item. `MATTER.md` and `OPEN_DIRECTIONS.md` amended in the reader's wording.

## 2026-11-07 — the record is a share of possibility, not a commitment; its amount is continuous (ruled, offered as a reading)

**The fork as left.** The trace a renewal leaves in the free stock is a which-way mark of size δ of one quantum. If the free stock is a continuous amount, the mark dephases at Γ ≈ δ²ω ≈ 10⁻⁴⁰ s⁻¹ per nucleon; if granular in ħ, "a share δ of one quantum" can only mean one quantum kept with chance δ, Γ ≈ δω, and heating excludes δ ≈ 10⁻³².

**Ruled, in CO words.** *The record is a share of possibility, left by a commitment, not a commitment itself; its amount is continuous.*

**Tightest form, checked against C3, C4 and P8 together.** Possibility is an amount; commitment is an act. ħ measures the act (C4: every commitment engages ħ), not the grain of the amount. The record is an amount: the share δħ·(P_free/P_max) of what a commitment engaged, kept rather than released (C3). It therefore has the continuity every amount of possibility has in the rules (P3's P_free = P(1 − h); P8's p₁, p₂, added as arbitrary reals), and the granularity of commitments does not apply to it.

- C3 defines the record as a share, a continuous function of P_free; nothing quantises it.
- C4 fixes what is engaged per commitment and says nothing about what is retained.
- P8 adds arbitrary continuous amounts; P3 makes P_free continuous.
- Nowhere in the table does possibility come in grains of ħ. The granular reading imported field theory's occupation numbers; the rules do not contain them.

**What it retires.** The granular branch of the fork, and with it the heating exclusion (Γ = δω) and the which-way problem for a pattern left alone: the trace remains a which-way mark, but a continuous one of size δ in amplitude, with overlap deficit of order δ² per renewal, Γ ≈ δ²ω ≈ 10⁻⁴⁰ s⁻¹ per nucleon (≈10⁻³⁴ s⁻¹ with coherent addition inside one hop cell). "Not resolved at any observable rate" stands unconditionally on the rules as read.

**Reading or new rule.** Offered as a reading: it adds no term and no constant; it states what kind of thing the record is, from the three rules that mention amounts, acts and the record. The one thing a reader may say it adds is "ħ is the unit of engagement, not a grain of possibility," and the question is whether that is already in the table (C4 as written is a statement about commitments) or is a new posit. This is to be pressed exactly.

**Grade.** Ruling, offered as a reading; candidate until the cold reading returns. If it stands, the conditional form in `MATTER.md`, `OPEN_DIRECTIONS.md`, the README and the plain doc is tightened.

**Cold reading, same day: reading plus one posit.** *Continuity is a reading.* The table already handles possibility as a continuous amount measured in units of ħ (P1 linear in ln P; P2 at most P_max; P3 h a fraction; P8 arbitrary reals; C3 itself keeps a fraction). C4 names the amount a commitment engages and never says the stock is counted in integers; if the stock were granular, C3 as written would be ill-formed. Continuity is the only reading on which C3 and C4 are both well-formed; the granular branch was an import (occupation numbers), never in the text. *The δ² rate does not follow from continuity.* Three cases for the free stock at a place: a sharp classical number, not a quantum system (no branching, Γ = 0, but the branched-field ruling is void); a sharp number in each branch (two branches differ by δħ·(P_free/P_max) at x, orthogonal environment states, every renewal a complete which-way mark, Γ ≈ ω, worse than the granular branch); an amplitude with spread σ in amount (deficit per renewal (δħ/σ)², Γ ≈ (δħ/σ)²ω). P8 fixes the arriving amount as a definite real and gives phase to transit, not spread to the stock at a place; nothing in the table sets σ. The author's δ²ω is the third case with σ = ħ silently chosen. **That is the posit, named: the free stock at a place is a quantum amplitude with a spread of order one quantum, not a sharp number (vacuum spread).** It is the one place ħ enters as a scale of the amount. Normalisation: coherent-state-like with n̄ = P_free/ħ quanta gives deficit δ²/(4n̄), smaller still; heating returns only if σ < ħ√δ ≈ 10⁻¹⁶ ħ, so the result is robust above that and the posit required is weak but real. Both branches of the old fork imported a quantum model of the vacuum; the reading removes one, the posit supplies the other. *P3 clashes with C3 and is amended:* "released when it completes; nothing is consumed" → "released when it completes, less the share C3 keeps as record; nothing is destroyed" (what P3 means is conservation; the free stock does go down, and the record counts as permanently engaged, which is what makes h grow). *Retirement half earned:* the granular branch and the Γ = δω heating exclusion are retired; the which-way mark is not retired but bounded at Γ ≈ (δħ/σ)²ω ≈ δ²ω ≈ 10⁻⁴⁰ s⁻¹ for σ ~ ħ. "Stands unconditionally" → stands on the rules as read plus the vacuum-spread posit. Method test for the posit: added where the primitives are silent (nothing sets the spread) and it retires a cost (without it the mark is total). Passes, named as a posit.

**Standing.** Ruled (the author's): the record is a share of possibility, continuous in amount; a reading of C3, C4, P8. Posited (candidate): vacuum spread, σ of order ħ. P3 amended. Results documents carry the no-collapse statement as resting on the vacuum-spread posit.

## 2026-11-07 — is the vacuum-spread posit already in the rules? Read against P1, P8, C5 and the Vacuum row. No; the sharp alternative is excluded, the classical one only by a ruling (candidate)

**The question.** The no-collapse statement rests on one posit: the free stock at a place is a quantum amplitude with a spread of order one quantum, not a sharp number. Does P8's phase, or anything else in the table, already give the free stock that character?

**What the rules say about free possibility.** Phase is matter's: "matter repeats, so it has a phase" and C5 gives it to a pattern in transit. The Vacuum row: "possibility with no commitment: no count, so no time; no repeat, so no scale." Free possibility has no cycle and therefore no phase. P8 adds routes of *transit*; it says nothing about the stock at a place. P1 makes hops compare the stock by ratio, which constrains how the stock moves, not whether its value is sharp. So the table gives the free stock an amount and a way of being compared, and nothing else. The posit is not already in the rules.

**A tempting reading, rejected.** "The vacuum has no scale, so its stock has no sharp value in units of ħ; spread is what scalelessness means for an amount." Scaleless means no preferred unit, not uncertain: a Newtonian potential is scaleless in exactly P1's sense and is sharp at every point. Wordplay; not used.

**The alternatives, and why each is closed.** With the record ruled a continuous share, the free stock at a place is one of three things:
1. *A sharp amount in each branch.* The branches then differ by a definite δħ·(P_free/P_max) at every place a renewal happened; the vacuum states are orthogonal after one renewal; every renewal is a complete which-way mark, Γ ≈ ω ≈ 10²⁴ s⁻¹. No electron would interfere. Excluded by every interferometer there is.
2. *A classical amount, the same in every branch.* No mark, Γ = 0; but the trace is set by where the pattern renews, and a branched pattern renews in different places in different branches, so a classical stock cannot hold its trace; it could hold only the branch-average, which is the mean-field sink, and that reopens the inconsistency closed this afternoon (a branched h fed by an unbranched sink). Inconsistent with the branched-field ruling and the trace.
3. *An amount with a spread σ.* Mark of size δħ against σ; Γ ≈ (δħ/σ)²ω; negligible for any σ ≳ 10⁻¹⁶ ħ.

Only the third is open. So the posit is **needed**, in the sector's sense: forced by the exclusion of the alternatives, not read from the primitives. Its size is not fixed: anything above ~10⁻¹⁶ ħ serves; "of order one quantum" is the natural value, not a derived one.

**What kind of thing this makes the vacuum.** Free possibility has no phase (no cycle) but is not a sharp amount: a quantity with a spread and no phase. That is new in the table, and it is the one statement in CO about what the vacuum is beyond "possibility with no commitment." It is not quantum mechanics' vacuum (which has phase structure through its modes); it is weaker. Whether it can be made to carry the tensor waves (the occupancies' radiative modes) is the unread item already noted.

**Grade.** Vacuum spread: needed (alternatives excluded), size open. Enters the results documents with that grade word in place of "candidate" after a cold reading.

**Cold reading, same day; enters as candidate, not needed; the posited object restated.**

- *Case 1 stands and is stronger than stated.* A sharp amount per branch, dented at resolution ℓ by each renewal (C1 engages *adjacent* free possibility), localises every pattern at resolution ℓ ≈ 10⁻¹⁸ m at its Compton rate: a momentum kick ~ħ/ℓ ≈ GeV per renewal. No atom survives. Excluded by atomic stability, not merely by interferometers; only superpositions separated by less than one hop cell are spared. Figures: Γ ≈ ω is 10²¹ s⁻¹ for an electron, 10²⁴ s⁻¹ for a nucleon (the entry paired the nucleon figure with the electron).
- *Case 2 missed its stochastic variant, and is closed by a ruling, not by observation.* 4a: a classical stock with one dent per renewal at the pattern's place, chosen by Born chance, *with* back-action: GRW at λ = ω, r_C = ℓ; heating ~10¹⁹ W per nucleon; excluded. 4b: the same dent, Born-weighted, *without* back-action on the pattern: the dents are uncorrelated with the branch, the stock holds no which-way information, fringes survive, and h is a classical stochastic field whose average is the mean-field sink; back-action through h is Δh ≈ δ/n̄ per renewal, unobservable. **Nothing observational excludes 4b.** It is excluded only by the branched-field ruling (candidate), whose own cold reading said the semiclassical alternative is viable for CO and that excluding it is a choice with content. So "a classical stock cannot hold a branched trace" assumed what it needed: that the trace is branched. (4b is nonlinear in |ψ|² and has no established dynamics: a cost, not an exclusion.)
- *Case 3 as written was not a coherent object.* Quantum overlap of two amount-states shifted by ε = δħ·(P_free/P_max) with width σ is e^{−ε²/4σ²}, deficit ≈ (ε/σ)²; classical distinguishability of two shifted distributions is ≈ ε²/2σ² per sample. The per-renewal numbers coincide and mean opposite things. If the spread is *ignorance* (a classical distribution over sharp values), then in each realisation the stock is sharp, the branches are orthogonal (case 1), and averaging zeros over realisations gives zero coherence: fringes gone, the information merely hard to read. For fringes to survive the stock at each place must be in a genuine quantum superposition over amounts, and a superposition over amounts has off-diagonal elements in the amount basis, a conjugate variable, which in field theory's words is the phase. "Spread and no phase" is therefore either classical ignorance (fails) or self-contradictory. **Withdrawn.** The posit, restated: *the free stock at a place is a quantum variable in a non-eigenstate of amount, width σ ≳ 10⁻¹⁶ ħ, with the conjugate variable that implies.* Further condition, unread: the Γ ≈ (ε/σ)²ω figure needs the stock's own evolution (ratio hopping, ∇² ln P) to act unitarily on that superposition, and a law in ln P acting on an amplitude is not defined in the table.
- *"Weaker than quantum mechanics' vacuum" withdrawn.* It is a mode at each place, in kind not weaker. And it is in tension with the gravity sector: if the free stock is slaved (a functional of the source), its state in each branch is fixed and it has no independent spread, and case 3 collapses to case 1. For a spread it must have an independent state and a conjugate variable at each place: a second dynamical field. The gravitational-wave derivation uses Lovelock with "the occupancies are the only dynamical field," met by the exclusion of a free-stock wave (R1). A quantum variable with a conjugate but no propagating mode is not impossible, but it has to be shown, and R1 has to be reread against it. Named as an open item. No direct clash with the tensor waves themselves (modes of occupancy, which has phase by C5/P6).
- *"Needed" not earned.* Exclusion (1) is sound and strong; exclusion (2) rests on a candidate ruling and a flagged choice; 4b is observationally open. A result built on a candidate cannot grade higher.

**Standing.** Vacuum spread: candidate. Sharp alternative excluded by atomic stability; classical and stochastic-classical alternatives excluded on the branched-field ruling only. Size open above ~10⁻¹⁶ ħ. Open items added: whether a free-stock variable with a conjugate but no propagating mode is consistent with R1 and Lovelock's second hypothesis; whether ratio hopping can act on a quantum state at all.

## 2026-11-07 — the free stock is classical: ruled, with the reason in the amplitude rule's scope (candidate ruling)

**The fork as left.** Vacuum spread is excluded on both sides (gravity record, same day). What remains for the trace: a classical stock.

**The ruling.** *The free stock has no amplitude, and so is not branched.* Reason, from the scope of the amplitude rule and not from preference: P8 gives amplitude, an amount with a phase, to possibility *in transit*, on routes; phase is matter's ("matter repeats, so it has a phase"; C5). Free possibility has no cycle, so no phase, so no amplitude: it is an amount. A quantity with no amplitude is not in superposition, so it is not branched. The branched-field ruling is thereby scoped: *a carried quantity is branched with what sets it, if what sets it has amplitude.* The which-way flux is set by transit (a pattern's sign on its routes) and is branched; helium stands. The free stock and the clock field h are the vacuum's, set by renewals and by draws, and are classical.

**The trace, on a classical stock.** A commitment is at a place; a renewal dents the free stock at the place where it happens, chosen by Born chance (|a|² there), and resolves nothing about the pattern (the 11-05 reading, held). The dents are a classical stochastic record of samples from |a|²; they are not correlated with any branch by a further posit, that each dent is an independent draw from |a|²; the stock's having one value does not give this on its own (a measurement record has one value and is branch-correlated); so they carry no which-way information and dim no fringe. Averaged over renewals the sink is the mean-field sink, σ ∝ ∫|a|², which is what the gravity sector has used throughout. Cost: a Born-weighted event with no back-action on the amplitude is a stochastic process nonlinear in |a|²; as a point process it is standard (a Cox process driven by |a|²); its nearest physical relative is Tilloy–Diósi 2016, with back-action kept; dropping the back-action moves CO out of the linear hybrid class (Oppenheim et al. 2023, decoherence–diffusion trade-off) into the nonlinear class, where the consistency question is Gisin's.

**What follows for gravity.** h is classical, fed by the stochastic classical sink whose average is the mean-field source. A superposed mass sources gravity by its mean, not per branch: the semiclassical side (Møller–Rosenfeld). The gravity cost (ii) of the branched-field entry reverses, and the sentence in `SPACE_AND_GRAVITY.md` with it. CO therefore stands on the side the gravitationally-mediated-entanglement proposals (Bose et al. 2017; Marletto–Vedral 2017) are designed to test against: it expects no entanglement through gravity. Semiclassical gravity is viable for CO only if the engagements that resolve a macroscopic body are commitments (the engagement criterion, open); renewals resolve nothing, so a pattern left alone sources h by its mean, which is the configuration Page–Geilker 1981 excluded. Page–Geilker's conservation objection to the collapse version applies to CO's Einstein–Hilbert exterior and is a cost. Known costs of the stance, to be read cold before entry: Schrödinger–Newton self-gravity (bounded, negligible for atoms; Großardt et al.); signalling in nonlinear-plus-collapse theories (Gisin 1990; Polchinski 1991), which is where a |a|²-dependent sink could fail.

**What it closes and what it leaves.** Closes: the record fork (count carried as phase; trace classical); the heating exclusion; the which-way problem for a pattern left alone (no branches of the stock to be entangled with). Leaves: the engagement criterion (unchanged, silence); the nonlinear stochastic sink's consistency; the no-signalling check.

**Grade.** Candidate ruling, the author's, with its reason in P8's scope. Enters the results documents after a cold reading of the semiclassical stance specifically: Page–Geilker, Schrödinger–Newton, signalling.

**Cold reading of the semiclassical stance, same day; pushed corrected.** The ruling stands and the BMV expectation is honest and testable; three sentences were not earned and one inference was invalid. (1) "No amplitude, so not branched" is invalid: a measurement record is a classical variable with no amplitude and perfect branch correlation. What makes the dents harmless is a separate posit, named: *each dent is an independent draw from |a|², not tied to where the pattern is later found.* Coherent as mathematics (a Cox process with intensity ω|a|²), to be named as a posit. Consequence: a pattern's successive renewals are i.i.d. draws across its whole support at 10²⁴ s⁻¹; no trajectory; "adjacent free possibility" means adjacent to the sampled place. (2) "Viable because commitments are collapse" not earned: a *renewal* resolves nothing, so a pattern left alone sources h by its mean, which is exactly the configuration Page–Geilker excluded; the rescue needs the engagements a macroscopic body undergoes to be commitments, which is the open engagement criterion. Page–Geilker's second objection, omitted: a mean source that jumps when a body is resolved is not conserved at the jump, and the Einstein–Hilbert cost requires ∇_μ⟨T^μν⟩ = 0; a cost on CO's exterior. (3) "No back-action on the amplitude" is false at the level that matters: back-action through h is Schrödinger–Newton at full G, not δ-suppressed (δ suppresses the per-dent noise, not the accumulated mean). The nonlinearity is Gisin's class; Bahrami et al. 2014 show SN plus instantaneous remote conditioning signals. CO's escape is the Entanglement reading (local source the unconditioned marginal until the far event is in the local past cone), but the plain doc also says "one event increments the count at both ends," and taken literally that returns the signal; the paragraph must not say both. (4) Oppenheim et al. 2023, absent: a classical field coupled to quantum matter by linear dynamics must carry noise with a decoherence–diffusion trade-off, visible in Cavendish experiments at CO's δ; CO evades it only by being nonlinear in |a|². Linear ⇒ Oppenheim's noise; nonlinear ⇒ Gisin: CO sits on the nonlinear side and pays with no-signalling. (5) "Bounded" for SN → untested where it would show (competes with dispersion only above ~10¹⁰ u). (6) Sink shot noise computed: ≈2 × 10⁻²⁶ rad per second on an electron clock from one nucleon's dents; energy kick 10⁻⁴⁵ J per dent; harmless, and classical, not the earlier which-way issue; the stochasticity buys nothing over the mean-field source. (7) ω|a|² is not covariant as an intensity (∫ψ†ψ = 1 in every frame, so a moving pattern would renew at ω in the lab, contradicting C2); the covariant form is (mc²/ħ) ψ̄ψ, which gives ω/γ; its positivity for positive-energy packets is to be checked before use. (8) Tilloy–Diósi 2016 is the nearest relative, with back-action kept. (9) Transit's draw on the sink (P6) must be said to be a draw or a functional of its branched amplitude; unsaid. Entanglement reading's double wording and items (7), (9) are open items.

**Standing.** Ruled (candidate): the free stock is classical, by the amplitude rule's scope. Posited (candidate, named): each dent an independent draw from |a|². Expectation: no entanglement through gravity, the asymmetry with the which-way field being the ruling's content. Costs open: no-signalling of the SN back-action (tied to the entanglement reading); conservation at a resolution event (Page–Geilker II); Oppenheim trade-off (CO nonlinear); covariant intensity; transit's draw.

## 2026-11-07 — engagement versus route, second reading: the two terms against the table (shape, sharpened)

**The condition set.** The engagement rule (a commitment engages another pattern's transit only through a committed pattern with an open channel, at Born's chance) stays a shape until "committed pattern" and "open channel" are terms in the rules table or are dropped. This reading asks what each could mean in the table's own words.

**"Committed pattern": redundant, and dropped.** The table already has the term: matter is "a commitment that keeps being fed" (C1); a pattern in transit is possibility between commitments. Every massive pattern is both, by turns: committed at each renewal, in transit between them. So the neutron crossing the crystal is as much committed world as the silicon it crosses (it renews at ω_n ≈ 10²⁴ s⁻¹), and the electron in a Bloch state as much as the lattice. "Contact with the committed world" cannot be the criterion, because in CO everything with mass is the committed world. The term adds nothing and is dropped. What it was reaching for is the next point.

**C1 as written feeds commitments from free stock only.** "A commitment, on completing, engages adjacent *free* possibility into its next commitment … it halts when none is." Transit is not free (P6). The only alternative C1 gives to engaging free stock is halting; engaging another pattern's transit is not in it. Today's vacuum is under capacity everywhere, so free stock is always adjacent, so by C1 a renewal never engages transit. The 11-05 measurement rule ("a commitment that engages possibility in transit resolves that pattern") is therefore not a reading of C1; it is a clause C1 does not contain. Stated as such: *measurement requires a clause, "a commitment may engage possibility in transit," which the table does not have.* This is the sharpest form of the silence so far: not "no rule says when" but "no rule says that it can."

**"Open channel": definable, but only after bound states.** In standard terms an open channel is an available transition: a final state the engager can take, energy conserved. In the table's terms: a commitment engages ħ (C4); possibility in transit carries ħω (P6); for a renewing pattern to take a quantum of another's transit, its own renewal rate must be able to change by that ω. So "open channel" = *the engager has a renewal rate available that differs from its present one by the transit's frequency.* The table has rates (C2) and an internal state (C6), but no spectrum of available rates for any pattern, because it has no bound states: hydrogen is not in `MATTER.md`, and a bound state needs two patterns coupled, which is today's candidate ruling (branched flux; Coulomb on the pair amplitude). So the term can enter the table only once bound states have, and then by identification: discrete renewal rates of a two-pattern bound state are the atom's levels, and an open channel is a level difference matching ω. Conditional, not yet a term.

**What remains after both terms.** Even with the clause added and the channel defined, the residual question is the measurement problem's own: *which* engagements through an open channel are irreversible commitments and which are coherent (Rayleigh and Thomson scattering absorb and re-emit through a channel coherently; resonance fluorescence has a coherent part). Standard physics answers by decoherence: the engagement is irreversible when the engager's new state is copied into enough further degrees of freedom. CO, as an event ontology, must posit where the irreversibility sits, and the three classes named earlier (rate law, threshold law, emergent) are still the choice. Nothing here chooses.

**What this reading changes.** The shape is sharper and shorter: *a commitment may engage possibility in transit (a clause C1 lacks); it does so only through a channel, a renewal rate available to the engager that differs by the transit's frequency (definable once bound states are; identification); which such engagements are irreversible is the posit still owed.* "Committed pattern" is gone from it.

**Grade.** Shape, sharpened. The shape line in `OPEN_DIRECTIONS.md` is amended; nothing enters a subject document.

**Cold reading, same day; enters corrected.** Conclusion stands (shape, sharpened; "committed pattern" dropped; measurement needs a clause C1 does not contain). Four corrections:

- *Premise and strength.* "Under capacity everywhere → free stock always adjacent" is invalid as written (under capacity means P_free < P_max, not P_free > 0; the plain doc's h → 1 body is C1's halt case). The premise needed is that free stock is nowhere exhausted where a pattern renews, which holds (the vacuum just under P_max; inside matter P_free = P(1 − h) > 0). And "by C1 a renewal never engages transit" was too strong: "engages adjacent free possibility" names the food and does not say "only"; "halts when none is" makes free stock *necessary*, not exclusive; C4 fixes the total engaged, not its source. Silence by omission, not exclusion; consistent with the bounded κ ≲ 10⁻²¹. Correct label: no rule *names* transit as something a commitment engages.
- *Channel too narrow, and unlike quantities set side by side.* C4's ħ is an action per commitment; P6's ħω is an energy; what changes at an engagement is the engager's *rate*, not its action per commitment. Restricting the channel to a renewal rate excludes every scattering channel: a free electron cannot change its renewal rate, yet Compton scattering off it is a measurement (Heisenberg's microscope); the engager's final state includes its transit (direction, P5). Channel, restated: *a final state of the engager, in renewal rate or transit, matching the quantum's frequency and direction.* Scattering channels are definable now; absorption channels need bound states, which are in the table at candidate grade and by identification only (the two-pattern ruling; helium), with the levels the standard construction's. Frequency conservation at the engagement is imported: the table conserves possibility, not frequency.
- *The residual was mislabelled, a category error in CO's own vocabulary.* Engagement is what a commitment does (C1), a commitment is irreversible by primitive, and C3 retains record from what was engaged; so every engagement is irreversible by definition, and "which engagements are irreversible and which coherent" is a contradiction. Correctly: *which contacts through a channel are engagements (commitments) and which are routes (coherent)*, as the 11-07 amendment to 11-05 already had it. Rayleigh (off-resonant, virtual, the atom ending where it began) and Thomson (a free charge, no level) do not pass the channel criterion and so are not examples of coherent passage *through* a channel; they show the criterion is a condition for absorption, not scattering. Resonance fluorescence is on-channel and still has a coherent part (Mollow).
- *P5.* "Transit keeps its direction until a commitment redirects it" is the table's only rule in which a commitment acts on transit, and it does not say whose. Read as the pattern's own renewal, nothing changes; if it admits another pattern's commitment redirecting transit coherently (Bragg, refraction), the table already has commitment *touching* transit without engaging it, and the missing clause is specifically *engage*, not *act on*. To be read against the Dirac walk.
- "At Born's chance" had been dropped from the shape without a word; restored.

**Standing.** Shape, sharpened: a commitment may engage possibility in transit (a clause C1 does not contain); only through a channel (a final state of the engager, in rate or transit, matching frequency and direction); at Born's chance; which contacts through a channel are engagements rather than routes is the posit owed. "Committed pattern" dropped. Plain doc line on measurement amended to say the clause is not in the rules.

## 2026-11-07 — P5: whose commitment redirects transit? Read against the Dirac walk. The pattern's own.

**The question.** P5: possibility in transit keeps its direction until a commitment redirects it. It is the table's only rule in which a commitment acts on transit, and it does not say whose. If another pattern's commitment can redirect transit, the table already has commitment touching transit without engaging it, and the clause measurement lacks is "engage" specifically; if only the pattern's own, the clause is any action at all.

**Reading.** In the matter sector the commitment that redirects a pattern is its own: the mass step (C6) flips the internal state, and in Feynman's checkerboard the flip is the reversal of direction, with amplitude i·mcℓ/ħ per hop. Between flips the pattern moves at c in a fixed direction; that is P5. So P5's "a commitment" is the pattern's own renewal, and the rule is C6 seen from the outside.

Other patterns act on transit in the table only through the fields their commitments set: the clock field h (refraction; the gravity rule) and the which-way ratio (charge). Those are routes: the transit's direction bends because the medium differs from place to place, not because a foreign commitment took hold of it. Bragg scattering of a neutron in a crystal and refraction of light in glass are of this kind. No rule in the table has one pattern's commitment act on another's transit directly.

**What follows.** The table distinguishes three things already: a pattern's own commitment redirects its transit (P5, C6); other patterns' commitments shape its route through the fields they set (P4, charge); and nothing engages another's transit. The clause measurement lacks is therefore the third, and the missing verb is *engage*: to take another pattern's possibility in transit into one's own next commitment (C1's sense), not to act on it. P5 is made explicit in the table: "its own commitment."

**Grade.** Reading; a clarification of P5 consistent with C6, entered as a parenthesis in the rules table. The clause itself ("a commitment may engage possibility in transit") is proposed as a ruling: the method admits it: the primitives are silent (no rule names transit as food, 11-07 cold reading) and it retires that silence, which left the 11-05 measurement reading without a clause. Not entered.

## 2026-11-07 — C8 entered (candidate ruling, the author's); hydrogen by identification; two small readings

**C8, measurement.** *A commitment may engage possibility in transit, taking it into its next commitment; the pattern whose transit it was is resolved by it.* Conditions carried from the engagement readings: only through a channel (a final state of the engager, in renewal rate or transit, matching the quantum's frequency and direction); at Born's chance (the possibility there). Admitted by the method because it retires a named silence: no rule said a commitment can engage transit, so the 11-05 measurement reading rested on a clause the table lacked (the cold reading of the same day: omission, not exclusion). It does not rule which contacts through a channel are engagements rather than routes; that is the posit owed, in one of the three classes (rate law, threshold law, emergent). Grade: candidate; its present support is that detectors exist.

**Hydrogen, by identification (candidate).** With the branched-field ruling, one electron on an external Z = 1 source has the Dirac–Coulomb operator as its continuum limit, by the same identification as helium; the levels are Dirac's (Sommerfeld fine structure) with 1/137 as input; nothing of them is CO's. Absorption channels are then level differences, which gives "open channel" its first concrete instance. Not entered as a result of the project; entered as the standard construction recognised.

**ψ̄ψ as the renewal intensity: positive globally, pointwise open.** The covariant intensity proposed for the dent process is (mc²/ħ)ψ̄ψ, which integrates to ω/γ and so slows a moving pattern's renewals as C2 requires. ψ̄ψ = |φ|² − |χ|² (large minus small components). For positive-energy states its integral is Σ_p (m/E_p)|c_p|² > 0, so the total rate is positive and correct; pointwise, |χ| ≈ (v/c)|φ| in the non-relativistic regime so ψ̄ψ > 0 there to order v²/c², but for relativistic superpositions ψ̄ψ(x) can be locally negative, and a Poisson intensity cannot be. So: usable as written in the non-relativistic regime, which is where every bound of this week lives; the covariant form of the renewal rate at relativistic speeds is an open item (candidates: the positive-energy projected density; the time component of the probability current ψ†ψ in the pattern's rest frame, carried along the worldline).

**Light's draw on the sink (P6): a tension found, not resolved.** P6: possibility in transit is not free and draws by ħω; light gravitates. Light never renews, so its draw cannot be the dent process. Under the branched-field ruling as scoped ("a carried quantity is branched with what sets it, if what sets it has amplitude"), the *occupancy* of a relation is set by transit, which has amplitude, so the occupancy is branched; and h is what the relations have delivered, which depends on occupancy. So h has two feeds: the renewal dents (classical, by the classical-stock ruling) and transit's draw (branched, by the ruling's own scope). That contradicts "h is classical" unless one of two things is ruled: (a) the draw is a share taken from the classical stock, and a share of a classical amount is classical, so transit draws by its mean |a|²ħω (semiclassical throughout; occupancy as an *amount* is the mean); or (b) the scope of branching is narrowed to the which-way flux alone, set by the pattern's *sign* and acting on other patterns' amplitudes (which helium requires), with occupancy and the stock classical (means). The two are the same stance with different reasons; neither is in the rules. Not ruled here; open for a ruling. Note that (a) needs the word "occupancy" to mean the mean amount in transit, and the tensor waves (mismatch of occupancies) are then classical, consistent with "whether the tensor waves are quantised is not settled."

**Cold reading of C8, hydrogen and the two readings, same day; corrected before push.**
- *Admission ground misdescribed.* "Retires a contradiction" contradicted the same day's cold reading ("silence by omission, not exclusion"). Corrected throughout: C8 retires a silence, which the method's test admits.
- *C8 against C4, and the quantum.* The commitment still engages ħ; part of it is a quantum of the transit rather than free stock; the engager's rate (C2) or transit (P5) changes by the quantum's frequency and direction. "May engage possibility in transit" → "a quantum of possibility in transit": an electron registered in a CCD is not taken whole into anything; it loses a quantum and continues. Cost named: possibility is conserved and sits at places; where the resolved pattern's possibility at other places of its support goes is not ruled ("one stock, cut once" asserts it; no rule moves possibility nonlocally).
- *"Born's chance" was ambiguous,* and under the wrong reading readmits the excluded Candidate 1 with a channel filter. Only "the place of an engagement, given one, is drawn from |a|²" is Born's. The chance per contact that a channel engages is the channel's coupling, imported from standard physics (with 1/137 in it). Silicon has an open absorption channel for thermal neutrons (σ_abs ≈ 0.17 b) yet is crossed coherently over 10 cm because the absorption mean free path is ~1 m; C8 is consistent with that only because σ_abs is small, and does not predict it. Bragg is protected for a different reason (the crystal ends where it began: no final state, no channel). "Outside a channel, none" made explicit; consistent with κ ≲ 10⁻²¹.
- *Hydrogen credited to a ruling it does not use.* One electron on a fixed classical source is the one-number case; Dirac–Coulomb follows without branching. External-source conditions named: no reduced mass (Rydberg off by m_e/m_p ≈ 5 × 10⁻⁴), no hyperfine, no Lamb shift, no recoil.
- *ψ̄ψ reading confirmed;* one candidate withdrawn: "ψ†ψ in the pattern's rest frame" has no meaning for a superposition of velocities; j^μu_μ along a worldline needs a velocity field the amplitude does not supply.
- *Light's draw: the two ways out both fail as reasons.* (a) "a share of a classical amount is classical" is the inference struck in the semiclassical cold reading (a record of an amplitude is a classical variable that records it), and it would make occupancy a mean while the ratio on the same relation is per configuration. (b) narrowing branching to the which-way flux names a case, not a rule. What remains is the dent posit extended to transit: P6's draw is an independent draw from |a|²ħω, not tied to where the quantum is later found, so h is fed classically by renewals and transit alike; one posit instead of two, and what "as a renewal would" in P6 already says. Not ruled; open for a ruling. The sentence above offering "two ways out" stands corrected by this paragraph.
- C1 in the plain doc now carries "(or, through a channel, a quantum in transit: C8)" so C1 and C8 do not read against each other.

## 2026-11-07 — light's draw: the dent posit extended to transit (ruled, candidate)

*Transit feeds h through its amount, |a|², not per configuration: light sources h by its mean, ħω|a|² being the energy-density component of ⟨T^μν⟩, and h is fed classically by renewals and transit alike. As one principle, on P7's two carried quantities: amounts (free stock, record, occupancy, h) are classical; the ratio (which way, set by a pattern's sign) is branched with its configuration.* This narrows the branched-field ruling's scope to the ratio, stated as a rule (P7's amount/ratio split; P1's "acts on amounts"), not a case. It is not the dent posit extended: light has no renewals, so there are no events whose correlation a posit could fix; and the covariant renewal intensity (mc²/ħ)ψ̄ψ is the trace of the matter stress tensor, which is zero for light. P6's "as a renewal would" fixes the magnitude, ħω, not the statistics. Chosen after alternative (a) failed as a reason (a share of a classical amount is not thereby unbranched); alternative (b), restated on the amount/ratio split, is a rule and is the one adopted. Costs. (i) Covariance: ħω|a|² is one component; the Einstein–Hilbert exterior needs the conserved, traceless T^μν = ħk^μk^ν|a|²/ω; with energy density alone radiation gives half Friedmann's coefficient, which BBN and the CMB exclude, so the radiation era stays not derived (pressure drawing, candidate). (ii) Matter's sink is the trace and light's draw the energy density; they agree in total only for stationary bound bodies (von Laue); light's coefficient (δ·P_free/P_max for matter, with no record for light) is owed. (iii) The mean source of a photon jumps where it is absorbed: Page–Geilker II at every detection, not confined to macroscopic bodies. (iv) A photon's mean source in superposition is untestable at any foreseeable sensitivity. (v) A sum of independent point draws (the matter dents) is not divergence-free point by point, so it can be the Einstein–Hilbert source only coarse-grained; a conservation cost beside Page–Geilker II, distinct from the shot-noise size. Grade: candidate ruling, the author's; a scope ruling, not an extension of the posit; entered in `OPEN_DIRECTIONS.md` with its cold reading still owed, which is the first item for the next session alongside the engagement-versus-route criterion.

**Cold reading of light's draw, same day; stands corrected.** The stance holds (light feeds h classically, by its mean); its statement did not. Light never renews, so there are no draw events to time (the phase does not advance along a null ray); ħω|a|² is T⁰⁰ in one frame, not a scalar; matter's covariant intensity (mc²/ħ)ψ̄ψ is the trace of its stress tensor, which for light is zero, so "as a renewal would" extended covariantly gives light no draw at all. Hence not one posit but a scope ruling, stated on P7's amount/ratio split. Light bending at 2× is untouched (light as a test body: γ = 1 from route lengthening); light as a *source* is where it bites: the radiation era needs ρ + 3p, Tolman–Ehrenfest–Podolsky beams need the full tensor.
