## 2026-11-15 — Tuning 1 and random births (desk)

The reading proposed for tuning 1 (places born at random through spacetime pick no frame) was already read and closed in the frame note (OPEN_DIRECTIONS_notes/frame_with_loops.md, "frame-free substrate: excluded (births, degree, locality)"; SPACE_AND_GRAVITY_record 7 Nov). Restated for the flow step: CO's birth events, at a fixed chance per place per tick, could be Poisson in spacetime and so frame-free as a set of events (Bombelli–Henson–Sorkin 2009), but matter does not move on births; it moves on persistent places joined to a bounded number of neighbours, and no finite-degree graph built from a sprinkling is Lorentz-invariant (the invariant neighbourhoods of a sprinkling are infinite). The flow step's generator is a nearest-neighbour Hamiltonian on such a graph, so the spatial lattice picks a rest frame however places are born. The flow step changes the frame problem's form, not its standing: with τ_f a convention, time is effectively continuous (a Hamiltonian lattice, as Kogut–Susskind), the cube symmetry removes the direction part of the speed counter-terms, and what remains is time against space, which only a symmetry mixing them would protect (hypercubic symmetry exists only on a Euclidean lattice; the frame note's real-time requirements stand). Tuning 1 stays: one counter-term per species and handedness, the same in every direction. The one door the frame note leaves open is unchanged (a network whose coordinates do not commute).

**Added (same day): the exclusion's other two legs.** Causal-set field theory propagates fields on a sprinkling without picking a frame, using kernels that reach every event in the past cone with weights fading along the invariant interval; the 12 Nov ruling allows fading reach, so bounded degree alone does not close the reading. Two further facts do. (1) Commitments cannot be the events: the vacuum holds none (7 Nov). (2) Birth events cannot be: at Λ ≈ 2 × 10⁻⁵⁷ per place per tick their spacetime spacing is Λ^(−1/4)·ℓ ≈ 5 × 10¹⁴ ℓ, about 10⁻¹⁷–10⁻¹⁶ m (a grain at 1–10 GeV), excluded by colliders by orders of magnitude. The exclusion stands on four legs: persistent places, bounded neighbours, no commitments in vacuum, births too sparse. Desk argument, not a theorem.

## 2026-11-15 — The bound from LHAASO's Cygnus X-3 photons; the photon test's mark

LHAASO's photons from Cygnus X-3 (arXiv:2512.16638; 3.73 ± 0.41 PeV and 3.08 ± 0.34 PeV at about 9 kpc) replace the 2.5 PeV photon: with the 1σ-low energy and the weakest direction, ℓ ≲ 2.4 × 10⁻³¹ m, δ ≳ 6 × 10⁻⁸ (needed). Above threshold the decay length is about ħc/(αE) times a power of (E − E_th)/E, so a photon could cross kiloparsecs only within about 10⁻²⁰ of threshold: the cutoff is sharp. The test's mark, added to README, ONE_PAGE and the prediction map after a cold read: a ceiling common to every source in a direction, whatever its distance (unlike microwave-background absorption, mean free path about 8–10 kpc near 2 PeV), varying across the sky by up to 1.75 (1.5 with the excess absorbed) in the cube's pattern. Exclusion from any direction above about 2.1 × 10¹⁷ eV; Auger seeing no photons separates nothing.

## 2026-11-16 — Neutrinos on the flow step (desk C4); the speed excess kept (ruled)

**Desk** (MATTER_runs/C4_neutrino_desk/). For E ≫ m the step gives E ≈ k(1 + rm/2) + m²/(2k) + (c₂/3)k³, so two neutrino mass states differ by ΔE = Δm²c⁴/(2E) + E·r(m₃ − m₂)c²/(2E_ℓ), isotropic, CPT-even, diagonal in the mass basis (checked on the exact dispersion along three directions to 5 × 10⁻⁵). As the isotropic dimension-four μτ coefficient, |c_μτ| = r(m₃ − m₂) sin 2θ₂₃/(4E_ℓ); IceCube's atmospheric ν_μ give |Re c̊⁽⁴⁾_μτ| < 2.7 × 10⁻²⁸ (90%), so ℓ ≲ 1.0 × 10⁻³² m, δ ≳ 3 × 10⁻⁵ at m₃ − m₂ = 0.041 eV; transfer of IceCube's fit (diagonal entries zero) good to about 15%. Conditional on neutrinos as patterns on the step with Dirac masses (the weak interaction absent). Neutrinos never outrun light or electrons, so superluminal-neutrino bounds do not apply. Cold read applied (Re/Im limits, diagonal remainder, mass cases labelled, time-of-flight figure per direction and reading, the reason pair emission is forbidden).

**Ruled.** The speed counter-terms do not absorb a massive pattern's excess s = ½ r·mcℓ/ħ. Absorbing it would need a counter-term per neutrino mass state cancelling their speed gap to about 5 × 10⁻²⁸, a tuning far finer than the 10⁻²⁰ the charged species need. With it: photon threshold C = 2.9–5.1, ceiling varying by up to 1.75, 9–16 PeV at the neutrino bound; candidate bound ℓ ≲ 1.0 × 10⁻³² m from neutrinos. Results docs, the photon note and a neutrino note (TESTS_FOR_EXPERIMENTERS/neutrino_flavour.md) updated.

Passed. Reading: the wall-bound matter of tuning 2's removal is one-handed at each wall in 3+1 dimensions, free fermions. Results docs updated.

Lowest level one-way on each wall, E/k_z = ±0.9997, opposite on the two walls and set by handedness; next level 0.3190 against √(2qB) = 0.3236; exactly one light state per wall. Passed. ## 2026-11-16 — The non-commuting door (desk)

Snyder's space keeps Lorentz symmetry exactly with discrete spatial spectra (so no loop splitting of species' speeds), its field theory partly regularized but with UV/IR mixing; κ-Poincaré deforms the symmetry (record above). Either needs places that are not definite in every direction, against P7, P2 and births per place, and either removes the photon ceiling (Snyder by exact symmetry, κ-Poincaré because a relativity principle forbids the decay at every energy); Snyder also removes the neutrino flavour term. Cold read applied. Reading: the exit from tuning is a re-foundation of place and costs CO its near-term tests. Note: OPEN_DIRECTIONS_notes/frame_with_loops.md. Grade: shape.

**Ruled (16 Nov).** The lattice's frame is kept, and the species' speed counter-terms are a standing choice, not a placeholder: the frame carries the photon ceiling and the neutrino flavour term, and the one open exit (non-commuting coordinates) would remove the photon ceiling (both forms), the neutrino term (Snyder's form), and places definite in every direction. Cold read applied. Results docs: "accepted for now" replaced by "chosen".

## 2026-11-21 — The wall's fade rate, exact (B7); the ceiling against sky directions (C5)

**B7.** At k = 0 the inner direction's zero modes are powers zⁿ with (1 − r)z² + 2(m + r)z − (1 + r) = 0 (one handedness) or (1 + r)z² − 2(m + r)z − (1 − r) = 0 (the other), m = ±M in the two halves; the leftover mass falls at the slower half's rate. At M = r = ½: κ = −ln(√7 − 2) = 0.4373 (the +M half), the other half ln√3 = 0.5493 with alternating sign. Against exact diagonalisation at four (M, r), rings of 24–40 places, the fits approach the exact rate from below: within 1% at three, 5% at M = 0.3 (still converging); the splitting is a·e^{−κ₊L} + b·e^{−κ₋L} with a ≈ −b, which is why short rings fit low. The +M half's closed form is the leftover rate for M ≲ 0.56 at r = ½. B3's and B4's 0.41 and 0.405 were short-ring fits mixing the two rates; corrected in the results docs. A larger wall mass is not always better: at M = 0.8, r = ½ the rate is 0.209 (the −M half).

**C5.** Photon bound by orientation, from Cygnus X-3 alone: 7.8 × 10⁻³² m (axis) to 2.4 × 10⁻³¹ m (body diagonal), median 1.3 × 10⁻³¹ m. A second unsplit photon adds at the earliest above 1.9 PeV (a source 55° or 125° from Cygnus; 2.1 PeV for the Crab's direction); none exists yet. At the candidate neutrino bound no orientation is constrained. Photon note and prediction map updated.

## 2026-11-21 — No pattern outruns straight light at r = ½ (scan C6)

Group speed |∇_k E| over the whole zone (161³ grid in one octant, refined), masses 0 to 0.5: at r = 0.5 and 0.55 the maximum is 1 (massless, k → 0) and below 1 for every mass; at r = 0.6 and 0.7 it exceeds 1 (threshold r = 1/√3). Confirms the low-momentum statement at every momentum. No doc change.

## 2026-11-21 — The neutrino term with three flavours (C7)

H(E) = U diag(m_i²/2E + κE m_i) U† + V_e, κ = r/(2E_ℓ), through the Earth's centre at mean density, 2–18 TeV. The μτ entry is c_μτ/κ = Σ U_μi U*_τi m_i: 0.02123 (normal, lightest 0; two-flavour value 0.02054), 0.01332 (normal, lightest 0.03 eV), −0.02415 (inverted). Matching CO's extra ν_μ disappearance to that of IceCube's limit (|Re c| = 2.7 × 10⁻²⁸, taken two-sided) gives ℓ ≲ 1.004, 1.600, 0.882 × 10⁻³² m, equal to the leading-order values to 0.1% on that path: while the phase is small (0.035–0.31 here) the disappearance depends on the μτ entry (its sign relative to the standard term included), so the diagonal remainder (C4's 15%) does not enter; the match there is close to forced. Cold read's reruns: antineutrinos and cos θ = −0.5 within 0.5%; near the horizon 1–5% (the μe entry is no longer matter-suppressed); opposite sign 1.3% at 18 TeV; above about 100 TeV single-energy matching fails. Not done: IceCube's own fit. Docs updated (inverted ordering 8.8 × 10⁻³³ m; the transfer about 1% through the centre, a few percent near the horizon).

## 2026-11-21 — Which extra species can rest (C8)

Near a zone corner with n components at π, E² = (2rn)² + (1 − 2r²n)q²_flipped + (1 + 2r²n)q²_unflipped + …: a minimum only if n < 1/(2r²). At r = ½: the three n = 1 corners (energy 2r = 1) are minima; the n = 2 corners are saddles (marginal at second order, quartic −(3/16)(q₁⁴ + q₂⁴) + q₁²q₂²/8); the n = 3 corner is a maximum. For r > 1/√2 none is a minimum. Grid search over the zone agrees at nine values of r. The corners remain stationary points with zero group speed; the "not particles" requirement and the 0.6 g fall concern the n = 1 species. MATTER and the appendix reworded.

## 2026-11-21 — The window for ℓ and what can close it (desk)

ℓ_min = ℓ_P√(4π) = 5.73 × 10⁻³⁵ m from δ ≤ 1. Neutrino term's floor |c_μτ| = 1.54 × 10⁻³⁰ (normal, lightest 0), 1.75 × 10⁻³⁰ (inverted), 9.7 × 10⁻³¹ (lightest 0.03 eV): 154–279 below IceCube's limit for lightest mass up to 0.03 eV (about 1.4 × 10⁻³¹ near KATRIN's limit); out of reach through the Earth (needs about 1 PeV). Cold read applied. The astrophysical flavour-mix searches are blind to it (diagonal in the mass basis). Photon ceiling at ℓ_min with the excess kept: 1.22, 1.65, 2.14 × 10¹⁷ eV (axis, face, body); the axis figure in the notes corrected from 1.1 (the absorbed reading's). Prediction map, neutrino note, photon note, ONE_PAGE updated.

## 2026-11-21 — Best walls, the ceiling's share of sky, the tick limit (C9)

**Walls.** At r = ½ the best equal walls are ±0.559 (rate 0.481); with unequal walls the −M half caps the rate at ln√3 = 0.549 (M₂ = r), reached once the + wall is near 1. Exact matrix agrees (0.477, 0.548 on rings of 20–32).
**Sky.** 18.8% of directions have a ceiling within 10% of the lowest, 44.4% within 25%, 7.4% within 10% of the highest; median 1.28 times the lowest.
**Tick.** Free step at r = ½: largest energy m + 6r exactly (for r ≲ 0.4 the largest energy is not at the corner). With a drift the shift term's size is |b_x| + |b_y| + |b_z|, not |b|: exact limit at τ_f = 0.4 is 5.14 along an axis, 3.17 along a body diagonal (the docs' 4.85 used |b|). **Corrected** in SPACE_AND_GRAVITY, OPEN_DIRECTIONS and the appendix. Runs against the true limit (cold read's recomputation): F2e (2D, cap 3, m = 0.5) largest energy 6.36 against π/τ = 7.85, within; F2f (1D, cap 3) 4.43, within. **Exception:** F1e–h (1D horizon runs) set the drift to √(400/6) = 8.16 inside ρ < 6, above the 1D limit 6.43 for ρ < 10 (largest energy 9.58). The evolution computed there is still exactly that of H (U is exp(−iτH) whatever τ); what fails for ρ < 10 is the step's reading as one tick with a single-valued energy. The measurements were at ρ ≥ 120, behind the absorber at ρ < 30 in F1f–g. Rerun with the drift capped at 4 (run F6): closed, see the gravity record. With m = 0.5 the exact uniform limits are 4.67 (axis) and 2.94 (body diagonal). Half-ring for 10⁻⁶ at the unequal walls is 26, not 25.

## 2026-11-21 — A box of trapped massless pattern ticks and weighs as mass (C10)

1D step, m = 0 inside a box of proper width 240, wall mass 1 outside; uniform field N = 1 − Φ, ψ² = 1 + Φ, coordinate width 240/ψ². Lowest level 0.006504 (hard-wall continuum π/2w = 0.006545). E/N against the no-field value: 0.99924, 0.99798, 0.99566 at Φ = 0.2, 0.5, 1.0 (N = 0.25 in the last); a rest mass alongside: 1.00000. Raised 50 places at g = 10⁻³: −5.19% against −5.00% (the width rounded to whole places accounts for the gap). Bag estimate: 3 × 2.04 ħc/R = 1440 MeV at 0.84 fm. Reading entered in how_patterns_combine.md: a nucleon as confined commitment. Walls held by hand; the scaling follows from P7f in the continuum.

**C10, inertia.** With H = (K − p)²/(2M_w) + H_box the box adds inertia 2S, where E(λ) = E₀ − λ²S under H_box − λp: S = 0.9968 E₀, so 1.99 E₀ (bouncing picture: round trip w/(1 − λ) + w/(1 + λ), E = E₀(1 − λ²)). Rigid walls give the weight E₀ but twice the inertia, the known box-of-light problem; real walls' stress restores E₀. Noted in how_patterns_combine.md.

## 2026-11-21 — Dark matter: the schools against the rules; why it would be cold (desk, C11)

**Schools.** MOND excluded (given the nonlinear rule's premises); ultralight bosons and axions excluded given the inventory; WIMPs, self-interacting dark matter and mixing sterile neutrinos not the strict shape; the far wall's mirror partners not cold dark matter (no mass source; if massless, radiation capped by the count of light species); the lattice's extra species excluded (they carry their species' channels); primordial black holes a silence, taken as not CO's dark matter. What remains: a channel-less fermion made at the beginning, m ≥ 0.13 keV.
**Cold.** Momentum redshifts with the light's: v ≈ 3.15 f kT_x/(mc), T_x ≈ 0.9 K. Cold enough for m ≳ 5 keV (galaxy counts) to 11–19 keV (Lyman-α) at f = 1; 5 GeV moves at 5 × 10⁻¹⁴ c; at 0.13 keV, 2 × 10⁻⁶ c, too fast unless f ≲ 0.01. Number per photon: 6 × 10⁻¹⁰ at 5 GeV, 3 × 10⁻⁴ at 10 keV. Sections added to dark_matter.md; cold reads applied.

## 2026-11-21 — The first ball's size; the dark pattern's mass by the count (desk, C12)

Visible universe: 1.47 × 10⁸⁹ photons, 8.94 × 10⁷⁹ baryons. First places (quarks, electrons, as many dark): 6.9 × 10⁸⁰; radius 5.5 μm (ℓ = 1.0 × 10⁻³² m) or 32 nm (ℓ = 5.7 × 10⁻³⁵ m). End of the epoch at 1–10 photons per place: 1.5–3.3 mm or 9–19 μm; growth 280–600 in length. Standard history back to the hop's energy: 4.7 mm or 27 μm. Dark pattern's mass under equal numbers: 5.03 GeV per baryon, 1.68 per quark, 1.30 per quark and electron together. The standard-history agreement is by construction (of order one photon per place at the hop's energy). Cold read applied. Entered as a shape in dark_matter.md and SPACE_AND_GRAVITY.

**C15a** (2D, massless, r = ½, 256², T = 150; bands stated in the script). Hop strengths uneven by ε per place, H = ½{w, H_W}, w = 1 + εη. Loss of the forward packet: ε = 0.1 at k = 0.295, 0.442, 0.589, 0.908: 0.0196, 0.0670, 0.1677, 0.4641; loss/(ε²k³T) = 0.51, 0.52, 0.55, 0.41. Power of k 3.09 (band 3.0 ± 0.6; pass); power of ε 2.04 (band 2.0 ± 0.3; pass). Born's value in 2D, ½ε²k³, is met to about 20% (cold read: the fixed window misses part of the scattering at low k and a time-independent offset adds back; five seeds give 0.51–0.58; the 3D limits are unaffected).
**C15b, c** (not registered). Seam between the square grid and one turned 45° (spacing √2, every other place). Every other row linked, strength g: turned back at k = 0.2: 9.4%, 4.2%, 3.5%, 3.6%, 4.6%, 6.5% for g = 0.7 to 2.0; at g = 1.2: 2.8% (k = 0.1), 7.4% (0.4), 57% (0.8). Every row linked (diagonal links g2), coarse scan: best g = 0.7, g2 = 1.0: 0.16%, 0.21%, 0.45%, 1.9% at k = 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8.
**C15d** (desk). 3D mean free path 2π/(ε²k⁴ℓ³), to a factor of order one: ε limits from a 290 TeV neutrino over 1.3 Gpc and a 3.08 PeV photon over 9 kpc, 4 × 10⁻⁴ to 2 × 10⁻⁹ by ℓ. Next-order scattering (k⁶ℓ⁵, not run): free paths beyond the distances for ℓ ≤ 10⁻³² m, marginal at 2.4 × 10⁻³¹ m.
Cold read applied: the seam's 3% is a long-wavelength floor, head-on, not a value at every wavelength (30° gives 4.5% at k = 0.1); a drawing wiggle needs the hop strength to follow the cell's size as well as the relation's length and direction, and its k⁶ℓ⁵ rate is a candidate, not run; limits tighten by ξ^{3/2} for unevenness alike over ξ places. Reading: the web may wiggle in its drawing; hop strengths must follow geometry hop for hop. Note: OPEN_DIRECTIONS_notes/the_web.md. ONE_PAGE and prediction_map now state the sky pattern's assumption.

## 2026-11-21 — The hand-drawn grid: can hop weights hide a wiggle? (C15e–h)

Places x_i = grid + independent displacements (0.15 hops rms per component), 2D, massless, r = 0. Sought: link vectors A_ij = −A_ji and cell sizes V_i with Σ_j A_ij = 0 and Σ_j A_ij (x_j − x_i)/2 = V_i·1, giving H = −iσ·A_ij/(2√(V_iV_j)), Hermitian and exact to first order.
**c15f.** Four neighbours: 6 conditions against 5 unknowns per place; least-squares leftover 0.023 and 0.067 per place at displacements 0.05 and 0.15. Eight neighbours: 9 unknowns; leftover 10⁻¹¹.
**c15g residual.** ‖(H − E)χ_k‖ on a dressed plane wave: four neighbours 0.07–0.09 k (first order); eight neighbours 0.17 k² (second order).
**c15g, c15h** (loss rate from the slope of the forward share; stated beforehand: felt-like weights k³, exact weights k⁵ and at least 5 times slower at k = 0.3). Four neighbours: 3.5 × 10⁻⁴, 1.2 × 10⁻³, 2.9 × 10⁻³, 5.1 × 10⁻³ at k = 0.33, 0.46, 0.59, 0.79 (power 3.6 here and 3.87 in c15g at L = 128, against the band 3.0 ± 0.6; the shares fall far enough that straight-line slopes understate, early slopes giving about 4.4). Eight neighbours, weights nearest the even grid's: 4.0 × 10⁻⁴, 1.5 × 10⁻³, 3.9 × 10⁻³, 5.3 × 10⁻³ (power 3.9; **fail as stated**, and not slower). With least-norm eight-neighbour weights (c15g) the even grid itself gains extra slow states and the loss is larger. Cause open. Candidates: at the wavelengths run the second-order leftover is as large as the first-order one (crossing near k = 0.5); scattering into the naive hops' doubled states, which first-order exactness does not suppress (the cold read's check at L = 24 finds most of the leftover at short wavelengths in both cases). c15e_firstpass.py kept: its cell-following operator was not Hermitian on the lattice (the product rule fails for central differences) and the packet decayed.
Reading: on a bare grid no weights of this form make patterns follow displaced positions (the count; one seed, 2D, r = 0); more relations per place cancel the first-order leftover; a lower loss was not found. Cold read applied: even weights that ignore the displacement hide it completely, so what is physical is the strengths, not the drawing. Note: the_web.md amended.

## 2026-11-21 — Smooth wiggles; setting the web even (C15i–k)

**c15i** (not registered; four neighbours, best weights, L = 96). Displacement 0.15 hops, alike over 0, 2, 4, 8 hops: conditions' leftover 6.9 × 10⁻², 1.3 × 10⁻³, 1.1 × 10⁻⁴, 8.8 × 10⁻⁶; loss rate at k = 0.33: 3.5 × 10⁻⁴, 4.0 × 10⁻⁶, 5.8 × 10⁻⁶, 8.7 × 10⁻⁶ (a floor near 5 × 10⁻⁶, not resolved). Displacement 0.6 alike over 4 hops: 3.7 × 10⁻⁵.
**c15j** (longer wavelength, L = 256). Four neighbours: loss rates 1.5 × 10⁻⁵, 3.5 × 10⁻⁵, 1.9 × 10⁻⁴ at k = 0.15, 0.20, 0.30. Eight neighbours: not completed (the weights' solve had not converged after 35 minutes; stopped). The payoff test stays open.
**c15k** (desk). 1. A local rule (each place nudging its own relations' weights to lessen its own leftover, cells held at 1): eight neighbours, leftover per place 0.21 → 0.12, 0.065, 0.031, 0.019 after 10, 100, 1000, 3000 rounds; four neighbours stuck at 0.14. Count with cells held: dz/2 weights against d + d² conditions per place, so z ≥ 2(1 + d): 6 neighbours in two dimensions, 8 in three (the cubic grid has 6). 2. Renewals per place over the universe's age: 2 × 10⁻²⁴ in the Earth and 10⁻⁵⁶ in a void (ℓ = 10⁻³² m); if each renewal reaches every place its pattern spans (6 × 10⁵⁰ for a nucleon), 10²⁷ in the Earth and 7 × 10⁻⁶ in a void, at either ℓ.
Reading: a wiggle smooth over a few hops is not felt as roughness on the bare grid (flat wiggles only; strains 0.05 and 0.105 against 0.21, so 16- and 4-fold of the 90- and 10-fold are strain alone; one seed, no even-grid control). Candidate: commitments are 4 to 5 orders too few to set the voids even (nucleons only, today's density, a renewal reaching a nucleon's own size; flips to about 10⁷ per place if a free nucleon's pattern spans its wave packet), so evenness would have to come with a place's birth. Cold read applied: the local rule's fall is slow (about n^−0.4) and steers toward drawn positions, which a backdrop-free web does not have; the count z ≥ 2(1 + d) is necessary only.

## 2026-11-21 — No drawing; flat or curved from the strengths alone (C15l)

**Reading.** With no backdrop a place has no position apart from the web; the geometry is the hop strengths. The displaced-grid runs (c15e–j) asked whether patterns can follow drawn positions; that question falls away, and the eight-neighbour payoff test is not pursued.
**c15l** (desk). Matter's step with hop lengths a_x, a_y: E² = Σ(k_i/a_i)², so ds² = a_x²dx² + a_y²dy², K = −(1/(a_x a_y))[∂_x(∂_x a_y/a_x) + ∂_y(∂_y a_x/a_y)], by differences. Rms K per hop², differences one hop wide (the first pass's two-hop differences were blind to hop-to-hop variation: cold read): flat by a smooth map (a = |1 + 0.1z|) 5 × 10⁻¹²; flat as a fan 10⁻¹⁶; bowl 9.3 × 10⁻⁵; ripple of 16 hops 1.6 × 10⁻²; independent 0.1 per place 0.54; a_x(x), a_y(y) independently uneven by 0.1: 0. Count: axis-link lengths alone (d per place) against d coordinates: always drawable flat; the right angles of matter's step supply the rest. Note the_web.md: "No drawing" made the lead; the flatness test added.
**c15m** (not registered; c15a's step and measure, ε = 0.1, T = 150). Strengths along x depending on x only, along y on y only: loss 0.0137 at k = 0.295 and 0.0454 at k = 0.589, against 0.0196 and 0.1677 for independent unevenness. Flat by c15l's test, still felt (cause found in c15n2: sizes drawn independently per place). The the_web.md paragraph and table on felt wiggles, dropped by mistake in the previous edit, are restored.

**c15n** (not registered; expected: link-sited at least 5 times slower at k = 0.3; met). 2D, massless, r = 0, L = 256, unevenness 0.1, lengths along x depending on x only and along y on y only. Links (V = (a_left + a_right)/2 per line, hop strength 1/√(VV′), c15f's exact first-order form, which closes for separately relabelled lines): 9.6 × 10⁻⁶, 7.2 × 10⁻⁵, 7.7 × 10⁻⁴. Ratio 30, 24, 4. Even grid: 10⁻¹⁵. One seed; the two cases draw different random numbers of the same size.

Lamé's six expressions for ds² = Σ a_i²dx_i², differences one hop wide, L = 32, rms of the largest: fan 1.2 × 10⁻¹⁶; spherical lines far from the centre 5.2 × 10⁻¹¹; inversion about a far point 1.1 × 10⁻⁸; lines relabelled alone (0.1) 0; bowl 3.8 × 10⁻⁴; ripple 1.1 × 10⁻²; independent 0.1: 0.45.

The link form with sizes drawn independently per place: 9.4 × 10⁻⁵, 1.3 × 10⁻³, 1.6 × 10⁻³. The gain is from V being the mean of the two links meeting at a place, not from where the strengths are written; c15n's first comparison mixed the two and its place case was not dressed (like for like the cold read finds ratios of about 22, 22, 4). c15o: only S's leading terms are one hop wide.
Reading: on an uneven web a place's size has to come from the relations meeting at it (candidate form of the step). Note: the_web.md.

**c15p** (c15n's web, r = ½; stated beforehand: G no faster than c15n's plain hops at the two longer wavelengths; U at least 10 times faster than G). **c15q** (2D, massless, r = ½, L = 256, the step ½{1/a_i, h_i}; stated beforehand: true path straight to 0.3 hop and speed within 0.5% of the even grid's, heading in the labels turning by more than 3°; all met). A bowl was also launched (heading +0.3°): its path passes near the bowl's centre, so it shows nothing either way.
Cold read applied: the leftovers (0.3–0.4% in speed, 0.10–0.18 hop sideways) are the size of the grid's own dispersion at k = 0.5 (plane-wave speed varies about 1% with angle and hop length; the fan's launch was 4.5° off the others in true heading, the map sits at 45° to its lines); the turn is measured over about 76 hops; a centroid sees neither loss nor spreading, and the fan's has a bias of σ²/2R ≈ 0.14 hop; c15q used lengths at places, whose lifting term (r/a) differs from c15p's (r/a²). Note: the_web.md (candidate form stated in the lead).
